# Video-game weapon inspirations

<!-- research-expansion:start -->
## New discoveries

**[146 additional gaming references](Video-Game-Weapon-Inspirations-Volume-2)** have been added in a dedicated illustrated continuation. The full gaming collection now contains **1038 references**, with all 892 originals retained below. [Historical additions](Historical-and-Experimental-Gun-Inspirations#new-research-additions) | [New media sources](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources-Expansion).
<!-- research-expansion:end -->


**892 references | 139 collections | 892 embedded images | 892 direct video references**

[Other collection](Historical-and-Experimental-Gun-Inspirations) | [Armory](Weapon-Overhaul) | [Reference library](Weapon-Reference-Library)

Weapon behavior describes the reference. **AoA remix** is an original proposal, not an implemented feature. Each image is embedded here. **Watch** opens its video directly, not a search. **Preview** means a video thumbnail rather than a separate weapon photograph. **Chapter** means the linked timestamp identifies the item; **collection** identifies an explicitly labeled multi-item showcase. Titles, sources and chapter metadata have been checked; this does not claim every video was watched in full. Source credits and detailed matching evidence remain in the [media ledger](https://github.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/blob/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/media.json).

| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/da352e06c107.webp?v=b3fa4e1" width="220" alt="Nasstod LD62"> | **Nasstod LD62**<br>A further mutated LD weapon form with a bright electric firing effect. | A branching sea-creature gun whose limbs synchronize for a charged burst. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=127s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-da352e06c107) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/8cced407c2e7.webp?v=c3c86c9" width="220" alt="Rusty Vito"> | **Rusty Vito**<br>A chunky heavy weapon with a distinctive mechanical silhouette. | A rusted reliquary cannon sheds metal flakes that become extra fragments. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=141s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-8cced407c2e7) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/512d07d8cdf6.webp?v=f49b596" width="220" alt="Rustier Vito"> | **Rustier Vito**<br>A heavy-weapon upgrade with an overbuilt profile and sustained fire. | A corroded barrel assembly opens vents between bursts to reveal a glowing core. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=159s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-512d07d8cdf6) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/7a037ccd6b28.webp?v=c9f9935" width="220" alt="Des Goules"> | **Des Goules**<br>A grotesque energy weapon with vivid supernatural projectiles. | A chained skull receiver charges purple motes into a narrow lance. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=177s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-7a037ccd6b28) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/a76a32d99164.webp?v=0e3defb" width="220" alt="Des Goules MkII"> | **Des Goules MkII**<br>A more heavily transformed Des Goules form with a powerful energy discharge. | A second spectral jaw grows behind the first and echoes the previous shot. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=196s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-a76a32d99164) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/23b297adf685.webp?v=e687f44" width="220" alt="Energy Booster"> | **Energy Booster**<br>A carved, otherworldly energy weapon firing bright supernatural effects. | A crystal-backed energy cannon whose apertures change shape with charge. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=216s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-23b297adf685) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/a10590a0e76e.webp?v=bc2af01" width="220" alt="Energy Catalyst"> | **Energy Catalyst**<br>An upgraded energy weapon with a pronounced organic structure. | Expose a crystalline spine while charging, then collapse it to release a pulse. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=233s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-a10590a0e76e) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/085f51003fdf.webp?v=086216d" width="220" alt="Worm Nest"> | **Worm Nest**<br>A nest-like weapon with a visibly biological firing structure. | Launch tiny spectral worms that burrow into a block and emerge toward a marked target. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=249s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-085f51003fdf) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/72103de69e18.webp?v=eb90d6d" width="220" alt="Big Worm Nest"> | **Big Worm Nest**<br>An expanded, more grotesque version of the Worm Nest. | A layered hive weapon whose chambers visibly empty and refill on reload. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=263s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-72103de69e18) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/620aa1fd6f97.webp?v=2073f3a" width="220" alt="Thunderonomicon"> | **Thunderonomicon**<br>A grasping, organic lightning weapon curls around a glowing core. | Turn different pages to route chain lightning through different target patterns. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=276s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-620aa1fd6f97) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/5025468bb019.webp?v=e0e4e10" width="220" alt="Thunderonomicon MkII"> | **Thunderonomicon MkII**<br>A grasping, organic lightning weapon curls around a glowing core. | The open book levitates a ring of pages that each become a lightning relay. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=291s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-5025468bb019) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/90cb574ba81f.webp?v=afbdce2" width="220" alt="Harpoon"> | **Harpoon**<br>A physical harpoon weapon whose silhouette contrasts with the occult arsenal. | An anchor gun plants a tether point and reels a spectral chain between targets. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=307s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-90cb574ba81f) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/fc2d33bc3429.webp?v=5b0777f" width="220" alt="Whale&#x27;s Bane"> | **Whale&#x27;s Bane**<br>An enlarged harpoon upgrade with a strongly nautical presentation. | A leviathan-hunting spear blossoms into barbed spectral ribs after impact. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=319s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-fc2d33bc3429) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/d21c72146df8.webp?v=9ee9153" width="220" alt="Aspergillum"> | **Aspergillum**<br>A holy-water ritual tool used by the Priest to stun enemies. A combat skill, not a conventional gun. | A holy-water censer throws droplets that become temporary damaging sigils. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=343s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-d21c72146df8) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/be3e53b47ec6.webp?v=89932fc" width="220" alt="Cross"> | **Cross**<br>The Priest raises a cross to heal. Its conspicuous hand-held ritual animation is useful inspiration for a healing combat tool. | A floating cross projects intersecting beams that form a temporary ward. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=354s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-be3e53b47ec6) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/6c110f752d77.webp?v=fb9aa59" width="220" alt="Camera"> | **Camera**<br>The Journalist uses a camera-flash skill to stun enemies. This is a combat tool rather than a projectile weapon. | Photograph a mob to store a shadow copy, then expose it as a short-lived decoy. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=366s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-6c110f752d77) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/236d40511d64.webp?v=589b946" width="220" alt="Voodoo Doll"> | **Voodoo Doll**<br>A humanoid doll acts as a physical focus for an occult ability. | Pin a marked doll to echo a weakened version of damage to its linked target. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=373s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-236d40511d64) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/c76f635ca81e.webp?v=918aca5" width="220" alt="Necronomicon"> | **Necronomicon**<br>A Priest skill granting temporary invulnerability. Study the activation and altered weapon appearance, not an assumed projectile-firing book. | A book summons ink-formed creatures from pages that visibly tear away. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=385s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-c76f635ca81e) |
| <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Herbertofury/Advent-of-Ascension/main/docs/weapon-inspirations/images/2305e5a995fd.webp?v=5e87400" width="220" alt="Mutated Knife"> | **Mutated Knife**<br>A transformed organic blade with a dramatically different silhouette. | A symbiotic knife extends a second living blade for an alternate slash. | [Watch](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L_59BErDMTI&t=498s) (chapter)<br>[Source](Weapon-Inspiration-Media-Sources#ref-2305e5a995fd) |
